| `EV-B0244-065` | `503013205:P15-P23` | Saori reports a message from Kajika that others want to see the group live and are apparently preparing an afterparty at Nanasta. Player raises fatigue. Jeda wants the unit to do more than one event, Sisara is willing if wanted, and Saori says she feels especially energetic without a new injury. Player proposes another live. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0136.json__d14b961d6d37bfc2?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`. |
| `EV-B0244-066` | `503013205:P24-P34` | Changed speech through Ei returns and self-identifies as Jacob, offering steps suited to a home party and audience enjoyment. Player's departure assumption is exposed as premature; Jeda reminds him it was his own claim. Sisara predicts Ei will also lack memory of the party. Saori calls that somewhat pitiable and proposes a cleansing, while Player remains unsure what would work. | `EV-B0245-063` | `504014705:P43–P56` | P43–56 the airport background accompanies retrospective camp conversation. Haru and Sumire say the stay passed quickly and was enjoyable; Momoka requests another next week without a booking. Coney and Player discuss a wake-up prank using a bazooka-cannon comparison, Kajika admits laughing and Hime comments on his expression. It is reported, not a viewed prank sequence or evidence of a functional weapon or injury. Player objects. Susu asks Musubi why she was not awakened; Musubi says she tried repeatedly, so Susu's accusation does not settle what Musubi did. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0151.json__7661f717bbeca30b?doc=primary&log=43#/Pages/43`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0151.json__7661f717bbeca30b?doc=primary&log=56#/Pages/56`. |
| `EV-B0245-064` | `504014705:P57–P73` | P57–73 the closing conversation differentiates experiences. Momoka wants a float next time, Kajika is enthusiastic, Sawara appraises Susu's sleeping face, Susu wonders about Player's response, and Sui adds a catlike comparison. These remain speakers' appraisals. Sumire asks to play with Shinju's hair again; Shinju agrees occasionally, and Rona proposes future ribbon shopping. Hime says she needs more dance practice, Shizuka offers to practise, and Musubi wants to join. Coney's claim that everyone grew and Player's agreement are local judgments. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0151.json__7661f717bbeca30b?doc=primary&log=57#/Pages/57`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0151.json__7661f717bbeca30b?doc=primary&log=73#/Pages/73`. |
| `EV-B0245-065` | `504014705:P74–P88` | P74–86 Coney converts those judged improvements into a proposal: return to Nanasta and surprise the fans with an unscheduled streamed live. The group is startled; Momoka objects to the suddenness, Sumire and Hime predict Coney will proceed, Musubi frames it as showing their lesson results, Haru rallies Player and he agrees. Coney addresses 777☆SISTERS and the group answers. White fade P87 and 終わり P88 close the presentation. The return and live are prospective; no streamed performance, audience, repertoire or technical outcome is shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0151.json__7661f717bbeca30b?doc=primary&log=74#/Pages/74`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0151.json__7661f717bbeca30b?doc=primary&log=88#/Pages/88`. |

Player's wording has a retained status difference:503013704/P26 calls the papers proposals for work he declined, whereas503013705/P18 says he had been thinking of declining. Both are his present accounts; no external company response is staged. The members' request to hear more and the costume trial are supported without silently converting either wording into a fully observed contractual event. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0141.json__398b818dcaac78bf?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`.

The cafe and appreciation party have retrospective completion reports; the camp prehistory ends at a candidate and differentiated anticipation. All seven older edge-ID gaps remain. The standing B0244 correction remains current: Musubi announces going home, while actual departure remains unresolved and later speech prevents treating an announcement as completed exit. The frozen B0244 reading and erratum are unchanged. A private B0246 static-description correction is separately retained: Miu's inspected skirt is dark and pleated; the original private case11 wording and freeze remain historical rather than silently overwritten.

## B0247 source-established collaboration, recognition and divided work

Owner [B0247](../02%20Readings/T7S_B0247_DEEP_READING.md), reading SHA256 `9c4c9b2a76cf7cfd93ad6aee35b34c55c346610d5f759a2e3e01cc26a16a3ff6`; source-lock12.156, witness T7S_GAME_OFFLINE_JA_R484, corpus c20260909-r484. Complete scope/limits: bound reading and AV-0194.

| ID | Complete primary facet | Source-established event and retained frame |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `EV-B0247-001` | `504016701:P0-10` | Player and Coney remark on an unusually peaceful Nanasta interval. Hime interrupts, calls the arriving pair something like a typhoon and distinguishes them from idols while saying she cannot forget their song. The chapter then uses a title transition; the calm-to-disturbance opening is explicit, whereas neither actual music nor the earlier performance is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0167.json__926a3fbc411ce755?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0167.json__926a3fbc411ce755?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`. |
| `EV-B0247-002` | `504016701:P11-22` | Separately labelled Hitoha and Futaba appraise the facility. Haru asks Musubi whether they are really KARAKURI; Musubi confirms her recognition. Coney greets the pair and says she previously saw them only on stage. Player asks Haru and Musubi what happened. Haru reports an abrupt visit/request to inspect Nanasta; Musubi reports trying to stop them and their forcing their way this far. Hitoha greets Player as shihainin/onii-san, Futaba separately addresses the manager/Coney. The approach and resistance are reports, not an independently depicted entry sequence. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0167.json__926a3fbc411ce755?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0167.json__926a3fbc411ce755?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`. |
| `EV-B0247-003` | `504016701:P23-33` | Hime wonders about a takeover. Player dismisses its economic attraction by praising KARAKURI sales at Nanasta expense, and Hime objects to that self-deprecation. Hitoha says they only want to observe Nanasta activities; Futaba calls this eras idol/Sister naming interesting. Musubi suspects teasing. Hitoha uses Musubi full name and claims prior research; Futaba separately names Haru/Hime and asks about Rona/Momoka. She offers wordless conversation as a way for the hosts not to be distracted. Claimed curiosity/research does not establish complete benign intent, consent to entry, omniscience or either mentioned members presence. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0167.json__926a3fbc411ce755?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0167.json__926a3fbc411ce755?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33`. |
| `EV-B0247-004` | `504016701:P34-39` | Player initially compares wordless exchange to telepathy. The earlier depicted family exchange supplied Yumeno wedding interpretation and Player corrections, not a mutually shown vow. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37`. |

That connection is Player explanation, supported by the earlier named inspiration but not supplied as Ferb own explicit reason here. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=44#/Pages/44`. |

A white-fade instruction, two unavailable command-only voice references and a final ending subtitle close the document. A capacity concern is stated; no actual crepe visit, complete menu consumption or rehearsal is shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`. |
| `EV-B0247-043` | `505017401:P8-19` | Anonymous classmate A, B and collective labels ask Ume and Hina to help their class festival stall, which is planned as a crepe shop. Ume immediately adopts a sugary public manner; Hina privately compares the switch to a ninja technique. Hina reacts to the crepe proposal, but Ume apologetically declines on the ground that 4U activities are busy. The source does not independently prove that scheduling ground false. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`. |
| `EV-B0247-044` | `505017401:P20-28` | Emoco enters and insults the cutesy refusal, telling Ume to agree to help. Ume anger breaks through, prompting a classmate question; she restores the public register and asks to speak with Emoco. She then objects to being ordered. Emoco describes issuing instructions to inferiors as an obligation of someone above; Ume challenges her leader/ruler posture. This is a contested authority claim, not a new established 4U leadership structure. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`. |
| `EV-B0247-045` | `505017401:P29-39` | Hina calls Emoco prioritizing a school event over 4U unusual. Emoco identifies herself as festival executive-committee chair. Ume is surprised; Hina first responds matter-of-factly and then registers delayed surprise. Asked why she accepted, Emoco says it fills time and is preferable to being ordered by someone else. Ume and Hina privately connect this with her dislike of commands; Ume recalls a teacher argument ending in the teacher nearly crying. That incident is her parenthetical recollection, not a newly depicted confrontation or an independently verified universal ability. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=39#/Pages/39`. |
| `EV-B0247-046` | `505017401:P40-51` | Emoco announces 4U full cooperation and offers Ume and Hina labor for the classmates to use. Ume protests, the classmates thank the two, and Hina accepts and proposes all three working hard together. Emoco then says her own demanding committee work prevents her from helping with this stall. Hina objects in dismay, Ume sighs, and a white-fade instruction ends the chapter. The opening promise has not established equal labor, completed work, a successful stall or cancellation of all band obligations. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0174.json__4b6704e0e8715943?doc=primary&log=51#/Pages/51`. |
| `EV-B0247-047` | `505017402:P0-5` | In the mall-background frame, Ume says she accepted the earlier assignment because it was in front of everyone but remains unconvinced. This supplies her own retrospective account of reluctant public acceptance at this chapter's horizon. Emoco dismisses her complaint as habitual yapping. Ume insists it is an objection to being ordered and asks Hina to support her; Hina answers with appreciation for crepes rather than the authority dispute. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=5#/Pages/5`. |

This is an articulated appreciation and procedural description, not an actually demonstrated cooking performance. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=6#/Pages/6`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`. |

Emoco has an exact written ellipsis at P22, whose native cue175062 is unavailable in the canonical registry. Ume enjoys seeing what she calls Emoco speechless face. No literal acoustic silence or settled private respect is established. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`. |

The conversation presents preferences and mismatched replies, not a class-approved menu, actual prices, prepared food or purchases. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`. |

Emoco uses a chick/feeding metaphor; Hina denies that reaction by saying an open mouth would let drool out. Ume and Emoco have a joint-labelled written ellipsis, and Hina says disappointment has made her hungry. No actual drooling, deprivation rule, completed stall work or resolved division of labor is shown before the final fade. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=39#/Pages/39`. |

Ume recommends preserving her condition; Emoco disparages that reasoning and forbids slacking. Ume privately reminds herself that classmates are present, repairs her public diction and invokes a hypothetical preparation injury. No actual injury or complete timetable is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0176.json__b65d6e3c3ec53c26?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0176.json__b65d6e3c3ec53c26?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`. |
| `EV-B0247-053` | `505017403:P16-26` | Anonymous classmate C asks whether an available Ume could appear in the drama club production. Emoco supports it through the barbed appraisal that acting is the only thing she does well. Ume senses the malice, nearly erupts, is checked by Hina and privately clicks her tongue before restoring the public manner. She welcomes the idea of shining onstage; C hopes her presence will attract attention and visitors. This is a proposal and audience expectation, not an already tested acting performance. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0176.json__b65d6e3c3ec53c26?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0176.json__b65d6e3c3ec53c26?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`. |
| `EV-B0247-054` | `505017403:P27-40` | C names Shakespeare and then King Lear. Ume privately thinks Shakespeare may be a famous play and does not know Lear, while publicly claiming it suits her. Emoco compares her to Lear eldest or second daughter, as a pointed casting appraisal. Ume invokes audience accessibility to steer toward something else, calling Lear by a riaju pun. C considers Lear too heavy and asks her to propose an alternative because she seems knowledgeable. Ume expands the expertise claim to backstage theater talk; Emoco says it is the first she has heard of that. These statements expose a local discrepancy between public confidence and the actual private knowledge shown, not a complete lifetime literary-ability diagnosis. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0176.json__b65d6e3c3ec53c26?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0176.json__b65d6e3c3ec53c26?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40`. |

Ume privately recognizes the title but is newly surprised by its connection to Shakespeare, then publicly treats it as what she meant all along. Emoco points back to the earlier very-funny description. | `EV-B0247-056` | `505017403:P51-60` | Ume treats the group as wanting her to play Juliet and promises a perfect Juliet. No classmate explicitly assigns that precise role in the shown exchange. Emoco suggests a fool would suit her better and insists that she memorize lines before the festival; Ume acknowledges the demand. Hina declares that all three now have tasks and encourages them. Emoco asks Ume to work for her share too, renewing Ume irritation before the fade. A theatrical commitment and expected memorization are established; final casting, script/adaptation, rehearsals and performance remain unresolved. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0176.json__b65d6e3c3ec53c26?doc=primary&log=51#/Pages/51`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0176.json__b65d6e3c3ec53c26?doc=primary&log=60#/Pages/60`. |
| `EV-B0247-057` | `505017404:P0-10` | In the sunset outdoor frame, Hina contrasts long preparation with the festival seeming to end almost immediately. Ume reports days spent with a long script and relief that this work is over. Hina reports happily eating many crepes; Emoco calls it eating rather than work, criticizes sampling every variety and says the stall nearly incurred a large loss. Hina calls it tasting and disputes the amount. Ume credits her appetizing eating with drawing customers; Emoco responds with a general image-strategy appraisal of the public. Actual consumption is supported through the reports, but quantity, receipts, exact cost, net profit/loss and a measured causal effect remain unverified. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0177.json__ae4c431b7ad81c94?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0177.json__ae4c431b7ad81c94?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`. |
| `EV-B0247-058` | `505017404:P11-22` | Hina explicitly praises Ume Juliet, supplying the casting/performance confirmation that Chapter 3 did not yet provide. Ume calls this natural talent. Emoco reports difficulty memorizing and near tears, which Ume denies; Hina reports stress-related sweets and Emoco says Hina accompanied her, adding body teasing. Ume repeats a Romeo line in the current conversation; Hina praises that scene, Emoco says she laughed intensely, and Ume identifies the remembered laugh as hers. Hina reports moved audience members and her own tears near the end. These appraisals and the present quoted line do not provide the complete earlier performance. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0177.json__ae4c431b7ad81c94?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0177.json__ae4c431b7ad81c94?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`. |
| `EV-B0247-059` | `505017404:P23-28` | Emoco describes the final feigned death as almost like sleep and says Ume did not wake for nearly five minutes. Ume begins explaining practice fatigue and release of tension near the ending; Hina asks whether she really slept. Ume then explicitly denies it and calls the pause acting to heighten the climax. Emoco calls the audience reaction a stir. The joke invites an accidental-sleep reading, but the factual account retains accusation, approximate reported duration, partial explanation, question and denial rather than an external confirmation. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0177.json__ae4c431b7ad81c94?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0177.json__ae4c431b7ad81c94?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`. |
| `EV-B0247-060` | `505017404:P29-37` | Emoco says she did not expect to remain so late and assumes a committee chair would be a nuisance at a wrap party. Hina compares formal live parties and Ume adds a food-related example; Emoco and Ume challenge each other's claimed restraint. Hina then reports that the committee welcomed Emoco participation. Ume disparages those willing to work with her, while Emoco says others filed papers when she gave a contemptuous look and adjusted her glasses. Hina connects this with recently observed frequent glasses gestures. These are retrospectively reported interactions and participant judgments, not newly depicted filing work, a clinical staff category or a current glasses asset. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0177.json__ae4c431b7ad81c94?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0177.json__ae4c431b7ad81c94?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37`. |
| `EV-B0247-061` | `505017404:P38-44` | Emoco asks why the other two did not leave when their class and theater groups had already dispersed. Ume says they stayed so Emoco could pay for a compensatory celebration after the trouble she caused them. Emoco says she has celebrated enough, while Hina frames it as a party for the three members of 4U and asks what to eat. Ume and Emoco jointly react to more eating. A fade and ending subtitle close the family.
